# Commands · E · part 3

1,410 entries, endpoint to epistract-domain-update. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **endpoint**: Scaffold a new .NET Minimal API endpoint vertical slice (endpoint + service + DTOs + xUnit stub) ([rprouse/dotnet-skills](https://github.com/rprouse/dotnet-skills/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/endpoint.md))
- **endpoint**: Add one API endpoint to an existing module, with schemas, service logic, tests, and OpenAPI examples. ([shahed-swe/ecomm-saas-marketplace](https://github.com/shahed-swe/ecomm-saas-marketplace/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/endpoint.md))
- **endpoint**: Generate a complete FastAPI CRUD endpoint with router, schemas, service layer, and tests ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/fastapi-backend/commands/endpoint.md))
- **endpoint**: Add one endpoint to an existing feature ([theodj97/CleanWebApiTemplate](https://github.com/theodj97/CleanWebApiTemplate/blob/HEAD/.opencode/commands/endpoint.md))
- **endpoint**: Add an endpoint across the API and its frontend ([UgoOluwa/HousingHub](https://github.com/UgoOluwa/HousingHub/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/endpoint.md))
- **endpoint-doctor**: Diagnose stalled tool calls on a non-Anthropic endpoint ([RichardRamirez123/NonAnthropicEndpointFix](https://github.com/RichardRamirez123/NonAnthropicEndpointFix/blob/HEAD/third-party-endpoint-doctor/commands/endpoint-doctor.md))
- **endpoint-implementation**: Twoim zadaniem jest zaimplementowanie akcji kontrolera w aplikacji Laravel + Inertia, bazując na dostarczonym planie. Twoim celem jest stworzenie solidnej i dobrze zorganizowanej implementacji, która zawiera odpowiednią walidację, obsługę błędów i podąża za wszystkimi logicznymi krokami opisanymi w planie. ([Wales86/fixflow](https://github.com/Wales86/fixflow/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/endpoint-implementation.md) · ★1)
- **endpoint-mapping**: Map a list of destination actions to their corresponding API endpoints, including field-level mappings, transformation logic, and gap analysis. ([segmentio/action-destinations](https://github.com/segmentio/action-destinations/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/endpoint-mapping.md) · ★83)
- **endpoint-new**: Adiciona novo endpoint seguindo o golden path (endpoints.ts → service → hook → invalidation → contract test) ([grasp-sitemas/alertport-admin-web](https://github.com/grasp-sitemas/alertport-admin-web/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/endpoint-new.md))
- **endpoint-plan**: Jesteś doświadczonym architektem oprogramowania, którego zadaniem jest stworzenie szczegółowego planu implementacji dla akcji kontrolera w aplikacji Laravel + Inertia. Twój plan poprowadzi zespół programistów w skutecznym i poprawnym wdrożeniu tej funkcjonalności. ([Wales86/fixflow](https://github.com/Wales86/fixflow/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/endpoint-plan.md) · ★1)
- **endpoint-test**: fixture — anchor carries a per-artifact relevance line ([adnanmokhtar/refract](https://github.com/adnanmokhtar/refract/blob/HEAD/tests/validators/audit-anchoring.sh/good/anchor-per-artifact/.claude/commands/endpoint-test.md) · ★2)
- **endpoint-test**: Hit a dev endpoint with curl and verify status + response shape via the endpoint-tester agent. ([adnanmokhtar/refract](https://github.com/adnanmokhtar/refract/blob/HEAD/templates/packs/backend/commands/endpoint-test.md) · ★2)
- **endpoint-test**: fixture — one global anchor block stamped onto every artifact ([adnanmokhtar/refract](https://github.com/adnanmokhtar/refract/blob/HEAD/tests/validators/audit-anchoring.sh/bad/anchor-global-constant/.claude/commands/endpoint-test.md) · ★2)

- **endpoints**: Probe the server's `GetEndpoints` reply. ([php-opcua/opcua-cli](https://github.com/php-opcua/opcua-cli/blob/HEAD/docs/commands/endpoints.md))
- **endSession**: **AUTOMATED SESSION END PROTOCOL** ([IcarusInHell/RP-Launcher](https://github.com/IcarusInHell/RP-Launcher/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/endSession.md) · ★7)
- **endsprint**: **Comprehensive Directory Scan**: ```bash # Execute full directory sweep ls -R . &gt; /tmp/full_directory_listing.txt git status --porcelain &gt; /tmp/git_status.txt ``` ([urbanscribe/artisan-claude-code-setup](https://github.com/urbanscribe/artisan-claude-code-setup/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/endsprint.md))
- **endtheday**: 1. Check for uncommited changes. If there are uncommited changes, ask if want to commit the changes or discard the changes. 2. If they commit the changes do this: 1. Ask if they want to make a pull request. 2. The commit message should be an abbreviated list of changed files. 3. Check for an existing pull request. 1. If there is one, push the changes. 2. If there isn't one, create a pull request a ([tjperry07/work_projects](https://github.com/tjperry07/work_projects/blob/HEAD/coalesce/cursor/commands/endtheday.md))
- **endurance**: Set or clear the long-running goal for this John session. Pinned into the system prompt by the SessionStart hook so it survives context compaction and stays visible across long ralph-loop runs. Use this command when the user says "let's run this in endurance mode", "set the endurance goal to ...", "/john:endurance ...", or starts any long-running shakedown / pipeline / build that should hold a finish line across many compactions. ([kitchen-engineer42/joharnessburg](https://github.com/kitchen-engineer42/joharnessburg/blob/HEAD/plugins/joharnessburg/commands/endurance.md) · ★9)
- **enemy**: Open an enemy lane session (enemies, bosses, final threat, concept art, 3D model and animation) ([twglhk/gamedev-agent-skills](https://github.com/twglhk/gamedev-agent-skills/blob/HEAD/commands/enemy.md))
- **eneo-commit**: Review the staged commit before it lands. Runs deterministic preflight + commit-message checks, invokes security-reviewer only on risky diffs, then commits through normal git hooks. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-commit.md))
- **eneo-discuss**: Socratic interview + scope lockdown for Deep-lane PRDs. Runs Pocock's interview script with the SuperClaude 90/70 confidence gate. Soft-requires plan mode; dispatches a read-only Explore subagent to verify assertions against the repo. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-discuss.md))
- **eneo-doctor**: Run environment diagnostics at any time. Prints the exact command to fix every issue it detects. First thing a new developer runs; first thing anyone runs when something feels off. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-doctor.md))
- **eneo-new**: Triage + entry point for Fast/Standard/Deep lanes. Classifies the change, writes .claude/state/current-task.json, and creates the right artifact (prompt / SPEC.md / PRD + GitHub issue) based on lane. Prompts only when classification is genuinely borderline. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-new.md))
- **eneo-peer-review**: Ask an outside peer reviewer (Codex if installed, else fresh-context Claude) to challenge the current diff or task. Plain-language input — the runner infers paths, focus, phase, and skepticism. Manual only; never an auto hook. Distinct from /eneo-verify gate 7 (automatic Borda) and /eneo-commit (commit-time safety). ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-review/commands/eneo-peer-review.md))
- **eneo-plan**: Turn an approved PRD into a tracer-bullet phased plan (Pocock template). Phase 1 is always the thinnest end-to-end path; phase count 3–6. Stop-hook validator enforces required sections. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-plan.md))
- **eneo-prune**: List stale skills (not triggered in 90 days) and recaps older than 6 months for archive decision. Never auto-deletes — always prints a review table and waits for the developer. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-prune.md))
- **eneo-recap**: Close the milestone. Only runs when all phases are shipped. Writes an agent-os-style milestone recap, closes the PRD issue with a recap link, archives the phases dir, and dispatches the learning-extractor subagent for skill candidates. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-recap.md))
- **eneo-ship**: Open a PR for the current phase via gh. Enforces tenancy/audit/PRD/phase metadata; Stop-hook validator fails the ship if any field is missing. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-ship.md))
- **eneo-start**: Resume the current plan and run its next incomplete phase via Wave → Checkpoint → Wave. Smart defaults; only prompts when there is real ambiguity. Emergency phase override via --phase. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-start.md))
- **eneo-verify**: Ratchet gate for the current phase — pyright strict, pytest, coverage, mutation ≥70%, audit-log completeness, tenancy smoke, conditional adversarial review on risky paths. Streams each gate as it runs. ([CCimen/eneoplugin](https://github.com/CCimen/eneoplugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/eneo-core/commands/eneo-verify.md))
- **energia**: Check-in de 1 minuto - sono, energia e humor de 1 a 5 e uma palavra do dia ([codetradeai/oficio](https://github.com/codetradeai/oficio/blob/HEAD/commands/energia.md) · ★2)
- **energy**: [Experimental] Detect ambiguity hotspots using token-level perplexity from a local language model. A second pair of eyes beyond the 31 rule-based metrics.
